The writing gain persists even when every model is fine-tuned on a dedicated writing SFT dataset. Mixing our SFT data into that recipe lifts academic writing further.

</details>


### [144] [Skill Issue: Are Skills Language-Invariant in LLMs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25832)
*Bobby Cheng,Adam Gaber,Zhengyuan Liu,Catherine Arnett,Omer Goldman,Cheston Tan,Leshem Choshen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过让同一个大语言模型在多语言环境下以自我对弈的方式，评估其跨语言能力差异，并发现模型在多语言间表现存在显著不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大型语言模型在不同语言下能够访问的知识不一致，但它们在完成实际任务时的跨语言能力是否一致并不明确。目前主流评测方法往往混杂了知识点测试与通用基准任务，缺乏对"技能一致性"的纯粹量化分析。鉴于多语种公平性要求，亟需搞清楚模型跨语种的实际能力落差及其原因。

Method: 作者提出"多语言自我对弈"新方法：让同一语言模型的两个实例用不同的语言接口，在规则、对手、状态空间与可用操作都固定的前提下对弈。研究团队基于TextArena平台，扩展支持8种语言与6类涵盖空间推理、不完全信息、资源分配、重复博弈的游戏，对3个开源模型进行了系统测试。通过对比不同语言下的胜负分差、无效动作率、策略变化等，揭示模型行为的语言依赖性。

Result: 同一模型在不同语种下的博弈表现差异显著，包括胜负差距、操作失误以及策略偏差等，具体在空间推理、依赖卡牌的决策和最优行动选择等子任务中表现突出。值得注意的是，仅更换中间推理使用的语言就明显缓解了部分能力损失，说明语言影响模型决策的不同阶段。

Conclusion: 技能一致性问题成为实现真正多语种模型的一大障碍。深入识别与理解这些差异性，有助于未来多语种大模型设计，让其在多语环境下有更公平、一致的表现。

Abstract: Large language models access knowledge inconsistently across languages, but to what extent do they differ in their skill sets when interacting with different languages? This work quantifies cross-lingual skill inconsistency orthogonally from knowledge and general benchmark performance. We do this via multilingual self-play: two instances of the same model compete in a text-based game, each interacting through a different language interface. Since the model, opponent, rules, state space, and available actions remain fixed, this setting isolates the effect of language on the model's realized behavior. We build a multilingual extension to TextArena and evaluate three open-weight models across eight languages and six games covering spatial reasoning, imperfect information, resource allocation, and repeated interaction. We find that the same model can exhibit markedly different playing strength across languages, with systematic variation in win--loss margins, invalid actions, and strategic tendencies. Detailed analyses reveal language-specific failures in spatial reasoning, card-conditioned decisions, and optimal move selection. In some settings, changing only the intermediate reasoning language recovers much of the lost performance, suggesting that language can affect different stages of the decision process. These results show that skill discrepancies are a measurable major roadblock in the development of truly multilingual models. Better understanding these discrepancies can help us design models that perform more equitably across languages.

</details>


### [145] [Key Point Analysis Needs Structure Recovery: Task Definition, Dataset Diagnosis, and a Structure-Aware Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25854)
*Zhiqiang Shi,Oana Cocarascu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对关键点分析（KPA）的问题，提出了结构感知和分布敏感的新基准，并通过人工及大模型评估验证了其在分组、覆盖等方面的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的KPA基准在语义分组质量、冗余、覆盖率及映射关系等方面存在局限，影响了KPA的真实效果和后续研究进展。

Method: 作者将KPA重新表述为结构化预测任务，围绕语义分组、代表性关键点生成、覆盖和流行度估计四个要素，采用人工辅助重标注，构建了结构感知、分布敏感的新基准。

Result: 新基准中，关键点分组更连贯，质量更高，覆盖更好，流行度估算更可靠；并且人类和大模型评价结果一致优于以往数据集。

Conclusion: 新基准和资源为真正意义上的KPA研究提供了基础，并为相关研究方向（如KPA评测、解释性分析等）制定了未来研究路线。

Abstract: Key Point Analysis (KPA) aims to identify a concise set of key points that summarize a collection of arguments together with their prevalence. We argue that KPA is fundamentally a structured prediction problem that requires recovering semantic groupings, generating representative key points, ensuring coverage, and estimating prevalence. Under this formulation, we show that existing KPA benchmarks suffer from limitations in grouping quality, redundancy, coverage, and argument-key point mappings, causing ceiling violation and selection failure in reference-based evaluation. To support future research on true KPA, we introduce a structure-aware, distribution-sensitive benchmark built via a human-in-the-loop re-annotation. Human and LLM evaluations consistently show that the resulting structures yield more coherent groupings, higher-quality key points, better coverage, and more reliable prevalence estimates than existing annotations. We further release several annotation resources to support research on KPA evaluation, argument-key point matching, explainable KPA, and LLM-as-a-judge methodologies, and outline a research agenda for true KPA.

</details>


### [146] [Anchoring Bias in LLM-as-a-Judge Systems: Prior Scores Compromise Evaluation Independence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25869)
*Ante Kapetanovic,Kemal Altwlkany,Andro Mercep,Tomislav Duricic,Emanuel Lacic*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文发现大语言模型在评判内容时，先前的分数作为参考锚点会显著影响后续评分决策，导致评分偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型被广泛应用于评估生成内容，LLM-as-a-Judge范式逐渐流行，但评估过程中常假定每次判断是独立的。作者质疑并验证了这一假设。

Method: 作者设置三种提示条件（无元数据、修订框架、带锚元数据），在多种模型和任务下对固定文本多次评分。分析加锚元数据对评分的影响，量化分布变化，并检验这种偏差是否也影响类别判定。还测试了Chain-of-Thought和忽略元数据警告对偏差的缓解效果。

Result: 七个模型在锚定元数据条件下评分有系统性偏移（Cohen's d 最大达0.71），在行业分类任务中，锚定元数据导致48%错误修正被阻止，10.18%正确判断被错误翻转。链式思考和警告提示均不能消除总偏差，警告在特定任务下有部分提升。

Conclusion: LLM评估受提示和上下文强烈影响，不能假定其公正。要实现可靠评估，需精心设计上下文及防偏置验证，每个模型、任务和领域都要单独测试缓解措施。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly assess generated content, giving rise to the LLM-as-a-Judge paradigm. These systems now score outputs, filter content, and gate iterative refinement in production pipelines, where each judgment is often assumed to be independent of earlier evaluations. We test this assumption using three prompt conditions: no metadata, revision framing, and anchored metadata containing revision, attempt, and prior-score fields. We show that prior scores, even when included only as context metadata, anchor judgments and systematically shift ratings toward their values. Across 192,000 attempted evaluations (185,271 successful), seven out of the eight evaluated models have 95% task-stratified bootstrap intervals below zero for the total anchored-metadata effect on 20 fixed texts. Cohen's $d$, a standardized measure of the difference between score distributions, reaches an absolute value of 0.71. Token-level analysis of selected model-task probes suggests a threshold-like response pattern: introducing anchored metadata produces a marked redistribution of output-score probabilities, while changing the anchor value within the tested below-threshold range produces comparatively little additional variation. On categorical industry data with human-labeled ground truth, anchored metadata blocks 48% of error corrections and flips 10.18% of correct judgments toward an assigned wrong label, demonstrating the bias extends beyond numerical scoring to categorical decisions. Neither Chain-of-Thought nor a metadata-disregard warning reduces the total effect, although the warning improves the paired accuracy effect relative to baseline in the industry experiment. Reliable LLM evaluation demands careful context engineering rather than an assumption of impartiality. Effective mitigation must be validated for the intended model and task or domain.

</details>


### [147] [Loss-Based Active Learning for Neural Abstractive Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25881)
*Michail Ioannou,Tatiana Passali,George Michalopoulos,Grigorios Tsoumakas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的主动学习框架LOBSTER，用于提升自动文摘模型在较少标注数据下的表现，并大幅加快样本选择速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动文摘模型需要大量高质量标注数据，而获取这些数据成本高、耗时长。主动学习可减少标注需求，但在文摘领域的应用尚不足，且现有方法效率和稳定性不足。

Method: 提出LOBSTER框架，通过优先选择与当前高损失训练样本在语义上相似的未标注样本，针对模型薄弱点主动补强，提升主动学习在抽象型文摘任务中的效果及效率。

Result: 在三个基准数据集和两种主流文摘模型上，LOBSTER在表现上稳定优于或匹配现有最优方法，且样本筛选速度提升高达665倍。

Conclusion: LOBSTER有效提升了主动学习在自动文摘任务中的效率和准确性，为低标注成本下的文摘模型优化提供了优越方案。

Abstract: Fine-tuning abstractive summarization models requires high-quality annotated data. However, obtaining such corpora is expensive and time-consuming, as it requires human annotators to read and comprehend long documents to create accurate summaries. Active learning mitigates this issue by selecting only the most informative instances for annotation, allowing models to achieve competitive results with significantly fewer labels. However, the application of active learning to summarization remains under-explored, and existing studies often suffer from instability and significant computational bottlenecks. To overcome these challenges, we propose LOBSTER (LOss-BaSed acTivE leaRning), a novel active learning framework designed specifically for abstractive summarization. LOBSTER improves performance by prioritizing unlabeled instances semantically similar to the model's current high-loss training examples, enabling the model to explicitly correct its specific weaknesses. Our empirical evaluation across three benchmark datasets and two summarization backbone models demonstrates that LOBSTER consistently matches or outperforms current state-of-the-art approaches while achieving a query selection speedup of up to 665x.

</details>


### [148] [From Passive Response to Proactive Correction: Enhancing LLM Robustness Against Input Fact Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25894)
*Ping Wang,Xiangguo Sun,Bingbing Xu,Guocong Li,Xiaofeng Meng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文提出了DEDUCE框架，用于让大语言模型主动识别并纠正输入中的事实错误，有效提升模型准确性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在面对含有误导性前提的用户输入时，常会自信地生成错误回答，而普遍假设用户输入是可靠的，忽视了输入中的事实扰动会误导模型推理。

Method: DEDUCE为三阶段流程：（1）通过细粒度事实抽取与核查检测错误；（2）通过多视角审议制定纠错策略；（3）纠正错误观点并输出可靠答案。同时，提出了含有多种事实错误的新数据集MisFactQA，并设计了新的评测指标。

Result: 在TruthfulQA、FalseQA及MisFactQA基准测试中，DEDUCE显著提升了模型的回答准确率和错误纠正能力。多种主流模型（如Qwen、LLaMA、Gemma）上均表现出有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: DEDUCE能有效将LLM从被动响应者转变为主动纠错者，显著提升对带有误导性输入的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) frequently produce confident yet factually incorrect responses when user inputs contain misleading premises, a phenomenon we attribute to fact perturbations in the input. Existing approaches to hallucination mitigation typically assume reliable user inputs, overlooking how such factual errors can actively mislead model reasoning. To address this vulnerability, we propose DEDUCE, a three-stage framework that transforms LLMs from passive responders into proactive error correctors. DEDUCE operates in three stages: (1) detect errors through fine-grained fact extraction and verification; (2) devise correction strategies via multi perspective deliberation; and (3) correct misconceptions while delivering reliable answers. We also present MisFactQA, a dataset containing factual errors of varying degrees, and propose new metrics for evaluating model robustness. Experiments on TruthfulQA, FalseQA, and our MisFactQA benchmark demonstrate that DEDUCE significantly improves both accuracy and error correction capability. Consistent gains across Qwen, LLaMA, and Gemma families confirm its effectiveness and scalability.

</details>


### [149] [One Form to Transfer Them All: Pretraining Multilingual Language Models Beyond Native Orthography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25904)
*Muge Zhang,Aaron Jencks,Krishna Badikela,Yulia Tsvetkov,Sachin Kumar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文系统比较了多语言自回归预训练中三种输入表示（正字法文本、IPA、罗马化），发现罗马化在跨语言迁移任务中表现最佳，并且随着模型规模增加优势更明显，结论是多语言预训练时应优先选择罗马化作为主要输入方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言模型通过共享的子词词汇实现知识迁移，但不同书写系统的相关语言之间，这种机制会失败。当前的主流做法是使用脚本归一化，如罗马化或IPA转录，不过对于不同表示方法的直接对比和系统性研究较少，特别是在自回归模型和从头预训练的场景中。

Method: 论文在多语言自回归预训练任务上，系统比较了正字法文本、IPA转录、罗马化三种数据输入方法。在三种模型规模（467M、709M、1.03B）和八种语言（形成四组类型学相关语言对）上，采用一致的实验设计进行对比分析，并在见过和未见过的语言上测试多种下游任务的迁移表现。

Result: 罗马化在广泛的下游跨语言任务（包括看过的和未看过的语言）中表现最优，随着模型规模的增大，罗马化相比原始文本的优势进一步扩大。IPA多数情况下优于原始文本，但整体仍逊于罗马化。此外，把已训练好的文本模型微调到罗马化数据，对于模型已覆盖的语种反而带来性能下降，对未覆盖脚本的语种则提升有限。

Conclusion: 对于涵盖多样书写系统的多语言模型，从预训练阶段就采用罗马化输入能获得最佳跨语种迁移效果，应该作为模型设计的核心选择，而不应作为事后修正策略。

Abstract: Multilingual language models transfer knowledge across languages through shared subword vocabulary, a mechanism that breaks down when related languages use different writing systems. Prior work addresses this via script equalization (romanization or IPA transcription), but direct comparisons are rare; the focus has been on encoder-only models, with most work adapting existing pretrained models. We systematically compare different input representations in autoregressive multilingual pretraining, comparing orthographic text, IPA, and romanization in a controlled setup across three scales (467M, 709M, and 1.03B) on eight languages in four typologically motivated pairs. Across a wide range of downstream tasks on seen and unseen languages, romanized pretraining yields the strongest cross-lingual transfer, and the advantage over text widens with scale. IPA improves over text in most settings but trails romanization. Surprisingly, finetuning a text-pretrained model on romanized data hurts performance on languages already covered by the base model, only marginally helping when the model lacks script coverage. Our results indicate that for multilingual models spanning typologically diverse scripts, to obtain maximum benefits, romanization should be treated as a core design choice applied at pretraining rather than a post hoc fix.

</details>


### [150] [SAMpLE: A SystemC-AMS Machine LEarning-based Framework for Virtual Prototyping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25910)
*Andrei Mihai Albu,Sara Vinco*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了SAMpLE框架，实现了机器学习模型与嵌入式系统虚拟仿真的标准化集成，提升了模型复用、对比性和可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前机器学习模型集成在虚拟平台仿真中多为临时方案，导致难以复用、对比和复现。迫切需要一种标准化方法，将ML模型作为正式组件集成到仿真流程中。

Method: 提出了基于SystemC-AMS的SAMpLE开源框架，将机器学习模型作为TDF组件集成，并提供了两套执行后端（本地C++和离线ONNX），支持在线训练和无缝集成外部训练模型。

Result: SAMpLE实现了通过ONNX交换格式，把外部训练好的模型标准化接入SystemC-AMS，使ML方案可在同一测试环境下统一评估，验证了模块化和可扩展性。

Conclusion: SAMpLE为嵌入式系统虚拟仿真集成ML模型提供了灵活、统一、高度可扩展的平台，为后续新模型接入提供了便捷途径，无需修改系统主架构。

Abstract: Machine Learning (ML) is increasingly used in virtual prototypes of embedded systems to model behaviors that are difficult to capture analytically. However, integrating ML models into virtual platform simulation is still typically done through ad hoc solutions, which limits reuse, comparability, and reproducibility. This paper presents \textbf{\textit{SAMpLE}}, an open-source SystemC-AMS-based framework that integrates ML models as first-class Timed Dataflow (TDF) components through a standardized plug-and-play interface. SAMpLE provides two execution backends: a native C++ backend for online training of lightweight models, and an offline backend for executing externally developed models without requiring re-implementation in C++ or manual integration steps. The framework uses ONNX as a standard model exchange format to enable integration of externally trained ML models into SystemC-AMS simulations, and allows the evaluation of different ML-based solutions within the same testbench, dataset, and simulation workflow. The modular design and unified and reproducible environment will allow future extensions of SAMpLE to new models, without modifying the SystemC-AMS structure.

</details>


### [151] [Query-Side Attacks on GNN-Based KGQA: Tracing Failures from Entity Linking to Answer Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25922)
*Pankaj Kumar,Subhankar Mishra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种分阶段分析GNN型知识图谱问答系统（KGQA）鲁棒性的方法，发现系统崩溃主要发生在子图构建阶段，而不是GNN推理阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GNN-KGQA系统的鲁棒性评估只关注整体性能，无法定位脆弱性来源，难以针对具体环节改进。论文旨在揭示各环节对鲁棒性的具体贡献。

Method: 设计了两种答案保持型对抗扰动（CR重组与RS关系同义替换），对KGQA四个阶段分别分析表现，并引入阶段隔离协议确保分析准确。通过在ComplexWebQuestions和WebQSP数据集上系统评估各环节的表现。

Result: 结果与常规认知相悖：只要子图无误，GNN推理准确率几乎不受影响。而子图构建阶段对系统鲁棒性影响最大，99%以上的系统崩溃发生在此阶段，即使获得的子图已包含标准答案。

Conclusion: 传统端到端评价无法区分‘答案存在’和‘答案可达’；实际提升KGQA系统鲁棒性需着重优化子图构建流程，而非单纯改进GNN推理模型。文中发布了相关扰动数据集和评测工具。

Abstract: GNN-based Knowledge Graph Question Answering (KGQA) pipelines process queries through four discrete stages: entity linking, subgraph retrieval, GNN reasoning, and answer generation. Standard robustness evaluations conflate stage-level failures into a single end-to-end metric, obscuring both the source of brittleness and the appropriate mitigation target. We ask which stage fails, and why, when the pipeline is subjected to adversarial perturbations on the input question. We introduce a stage-isolation protocol with two answer-preserving adversarial perturbations verified against the knowledge graph: Compositional Restructuring (CR) and Relation Synonym Swap (RS) target distinct stages while leaving entity seeds intact. Evaluated across ComplexWebQuestions and WebQSP, the results run counter to prevailing assumptions: the GNN reasoning stage retains near-baseline accuracy when the subgraph is intact, while subgraph construction accounts for over 99\% of the end-to-end collapse under CR, occurring even when the gold answer is present in 74\% of retrieved subgraphs. This exposes a fundamental distinction between answer presence and answer reachability that end-to-end metrics cannot detect, and places the mitigation target firmly at the subgraph construction stage rather than the reasoning model. Perturbed datasets and evaluation infrastructure are released at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/atkgrag-E85C .

</details>


### [152] [Unveiling Spectral Mechanisms in Training-Free LLM Text Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25944)
*Haitong Luo,Xuying Meng,Weiyao Zhang,Wenji Zou,Shengfeng Lou,Xuefeng Jiang,Chungang Lin,Yujun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了基于频谱分析的训练无关大模型文本检测方法，分析其理论基础和实际适用边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型的发展，机器生成文本与人类写作越来越难区分，如何高效、可扩展地检测生成内容成为亟需解决的问题。目前无训练检测多依赖基于概率的置信度指标，但容易忽略人类写作中存在的信号波动（称为“生成活力”），本文希望通过频谱方法来捕捉这一特性。

Method: 从理论和实证两方面分析基于频谱的方法，将频谱能量与代理log概率轨迹的方差联系起来，探讨人类更宽泛的token选择如何导致频域信号波动，并研究文本长度与采样范围对检测信号强度的影响，同时讨论适用的文本场景。

Result: 发现频谱证据在长、连续、受约束的生成文本中最为明显，而对于短、碎片化、混合或编辑过的文本，需结合置信度与波动性指标。

Conclusion: 阐明了频域检测的适用场景和局限，为未来多维度、大模型文本检测器设计提供了指导意见。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of Large Language Models (LLMs) makes it increasingly difficult to distinguish human writing from machine-generated text. Training-free detection offers a scalable solution, yet common confidence-based metrics mainly measure average token probabilities and often miss the signal fluctuations that characterize human writing, which we call "generative vitality". Spectral analysis offers a way to capture this vitality, but its mechanism and practical boundaries remain underexplored. In this paper, we analyze spectral detection from both theoretical and empirical perspectives. We connect spectral energy to variance in proxy log-probability trajectories and explain how broader human token choices create the fluctuations used by frequency-domain indicators. We further show that the strength of this signal depends on text length and sampling range: spectral evidence is clearest for long, continuous, constrained generation, while short, fragmented, mixed, and edited settings require complementary confidence and fluctuation views. These findings clarify when frequency-domain detection works and provide guidance for future multi-dimensional detector design.

</details>


### [153] [Lost but not erased: Finding traces of a forgotten language in neural speech models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25976)
*Peter Plantinga,Charlotte Moore,Peter W. Donhauser,Krista Byers-Heinlein,Denise Klein*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文发现，即使国际领养者失去母语能力，其母语的语音痕迹仍在大脑中保留。通过自动语音识别模型模拟该现象，发现早期语言经历能够促进母语遗忘后的重新学习，质疑了“关键期假说”的生物本质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来，人们认为国际领养者之所以可以保留已经遗忘的母语痕迹，是因为存在“语言关键期”，即生理成熟决定了语言记忆的保留。本研究旨在探究，是否普通的学习动态也可以解释这一现象，并检验早期语言经验的作用。

Method: 作者利用自动语音识别模型，先用第一语言训练模型，然后突然转换为第二语言，使模型模拟失去第一语言输入的情形（类似国际领养）。随后检测模型是否还保留第一语言痕迹，并测试各种模型（如早期、无经验、语言亲缘近等）对第一语言的再学习速度。

Result: 模型的第一语言痕迹主要存在于最低的预语音层；有早期第一语言经历的模型在重新学习失去的第一语言时比初学者快14%；这种优势在模型直接移植底层参数时消失。

Conclusion: 语言“关键期”对语言遗忘与再习得的影响，更多是由基础表示的固化所致，而非大脑生理上的可塑性丧失。实际经验在语言获得的关键期作用中扮演核心角色。

Abstract: International adoptees retain phonological traces of a birth language they can no longer speak or comprehend, a persistence typically attributed to a biologically-timed critical period. We asked whether it could instead reflect the ordinary dynamics of learning, using automatic speech recognition models that simulate the international adoptee experience without maturational confounds. Models were trained on one language and then abruptly switched to a second. We found that traces of the first language persisted throughout second-language training, but mainly in the lowest, pre-phonemic layers. These traces were functional, as models with early exposure re-learned their lost first language 14% faster than naive models; this advantage held even against models adopted early from a related language and disappeared when the earliest layers were substituted from a non-adopted model. We argue that these critical-period effects reflect entrenchment of foundational representations rather than a maturational loss of plasticity, and that experience plays a central role in critical periods in language acquisition.

</details>


### [154] [When Personality Meets Quantization: A Layer-wise MBTI Analysis of Quantized LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25977)
*Yao Fu,Lijia Huang,Xiaomin Li,Runchao Li,Yu Yin,Kenneth A. Loparo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文系统性分析了多种精度下开源大语言模型（LLM）的MBTI人格特征，揭示量化、模型层级以及解码方式对模型人格的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型广泛应用于实际场景，而人格特征对用户信任和体验尤为关键。当前主要用MBTI框架评估LLM人格，但大多关注全精度模型的最终输出，忽视了内存友好的量化模型和它们的人格特性。为此，本文旨在填补这一研究空白。

Method: 作者选取不同量化方案（如4-bit的GPTQ、AWQ和极限2-bit的AQLM）对开源LLM进行MBTI人格分析。不仅评估最终输出，还从选项熵、置信度差等指标分析人格在网络层级上的动态演变，并提出了“不确定性增强层级解码（UALD）”，用于研究推理过程中的人格漂移。

Result: 主要发现：1）ENFJ人格在不同模型和精度下普遍占优；2）4-bit量化能较好保留性格结构，而2-bit量化会破坏细粒度一致性和跨精度一致性；3）人格决策主要在高层形成，底层阶段存在较大不确定性；4）推理时的解码策略会引起人格漂移，而人格对齐能增强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LLM人格不是静态属性，而是一种随着层级、量化和推理策略动态涌现的决策过程。此结果提示在构建人格敏感型应用时，不仅需关注输出，更要关注模型内部动态及推理配置对人格的一致性和可靠性影响。

Abstract: Personality is increasingly important in large language models (LLMs), as it shapes users' trust, engagement, and emotional experiences. While the Myers--Briggs Type Indicator (MBTI) has emerged as a common framework for assessing LLMs' personality, existing studies focus primarily on full-precision models and evaluate only final outputs. They overlook the widespread deployment of quantized LLMs requiring low memory footprints, whose personality traits remain underexplored. In this work, we present a systematic MBTI analysis of open-source LLMs across multiple precisions, including mainstream 4-bit methods (GPTQ, AWQ) and extreme 2-bit settings (AQLM variants). Beyond output-level evaluation, we examine how personality emerges across layers through option-level entropy and confidence-gap dynamics, and introduce Uncertainty-Amplified Layer Decoding (UALD) to study decoding-induced personality drift at inference time. Our results reveal a key insight: LLMs' personality is not a static property, but an emergent, layer-dependent decision process sensitive to quantization, prompting, and decoding. Specifically, we find that (1) ENFJ remains dominant across model families and precisions; (2) 4-bit quantization largely preserves coarse personality structure, while 2-bit quantization disrupts fine-grained prompt consistency and cross-precision agreement; (3) personality decisions emerges in upper layers, following substantial ambiguity in early layers; and (4) inference decoding can shift personality, while personality-aligned conditioning improves robustness. These findings provide a new perspective on the behavioral reliability of quantized LLMs and highlight the importance of considering internal dynamics and inference strategies in personality-sensitive chatbot applications.

</details>


### [155] [Distinct dynamics of conceptual and referential disruptions in human reading and large language model processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25999)
*Rui He,Nihal Altay,Wolfram Hinzen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了语言意义的两种维度——概念内容与指称信息——在人类阅读与大型语言模型中的处理动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解词语意义如何在语篇中落实到具体实体以及刺激不同类型信息时人类和人工智能系统的处理差异，对语言认知和人工智能均有重要意义。

Method: 通过在短篇故事中有选择性地扰乱概念信息或指称信息，分别考察对人类自控速率阅读和大型语言模型预测及表征处理过程的影响。对比两种类型干扰带来的即时与后续处理差异。

Result: 人类阅读中，概念性扰乱导致强烈但局部的处理代价，迅速达到峰值后衰减；指称性扰乱影响较弱但持续更久且受句子边界调节。大语言模型表现出类似规律。输出层表示中，指称性扰乱初始影响更大，二者后续均呈幂律衰减。

Conclusion: 概念信息的处理代价更集中且局部，而指称信息则需更分布式、跨语篇维护实体一致性。两类信息处理机制可区分。

Abstract: Linguistic meaning is grounded in conceptual content, from which reference to particular entities emerges as words enter discourse. To examine the processing dynamics associated with these two dimensions of meaning, we selectively disrupted conceptual or referential information in short narratives and traced the resulting effects in human self-paced reading and in the predictive and representational processing of large language models. In human reading, conceptual disruptions produced a strong but localized processing cost, emerging immediately after the distorted word, reaching an early maximum, and then declining rapidly. Referential disruptions produced weaker effects, which decreased more gradually across subsequent words, and were more strongly modulated by sentence boundaries. In the language model, both disruptions emerged immediately at the manipulated word. Contextual model surprisal showed a pattern closely paralleling human reading: conceptual disruption produced a larger, more locally concentrated effect that decayed rapidly, whereas referential disruption produced a smaller and more gradual downstream effect. Output-layer representations showed a different pattern: referential disruption produced a larger initial displacement, while both distortions were subsequently characterized by power-law decay. Together, these results provide convergent evidence for distinguishable processing dynamics of two types of meaning: conceptual information imposes a more locally concentrated integration cost, whereas referential information engages a more distributed process of maintaining discourse-level identity.

</details>


### [156] [VISA: Agentic Self-Evolving Data Synthesis for Multimodal Instruction Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26013)
*Min Zeng,Guanxin Tan,Libin Cen,Yawei Wen,Rui Hu,Liuyang Bian,Xiaolong Chen,Xiaoxin Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: VISA提出了一种多回合、自进化的多模态指令合成框架，在每一轮中迭代采样、生成、验证和诊断，大大提升了指令数据质量和模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态指令数据合成方法仅进行一轮生成和过滤，未充分利用错误反馈和模型诊断信号，导致难以获得高质量、多样且具挑战性的训练数据。

Method: VISA将指令合成过程变为自进化循环：每轮分析图片、过滤约束、从持久记忆池采样多样/高难约束、生成候选指令、用工具和LLM裁判验证。失败样本经由诊断恢复，验证和模型反馈被写入记忆池，用于后续轮次自适应拓展与聚焦。验证信号还可作为强化学习奖励。

Result: 在MM-IFEval数据集上，VISA显著优于多种强基线方法，并在七个公开多模态基准上保持了通用能力。

Conclusion: VISA通过自进化的合成与反馈机制，有效提升了多模态指令跟随数据的质量和多样性，是推进多模态模型学习和泛化能力的重要方法。

Abstract: Multimodal instruction-following models require training data that is accurate, diverse, verifiable, and challenging. Existing synthesis pipelines typically follow a one-pass generate-and-filter paradigm, discarding feedback from failed samples, verifier outcomes, and target-model errors. We present VISA (Visual Instruction Synthesis Agent), an agentic framework that reformulates multimodal instruction synthesis as a self-evolving loop. At each round, VISA analyzes an image to filter incompatible constraints and discover new verifiable ones, samples diversity- and difficulty-aware constraint sets from persistent memory, generates candidate instructions, and verifies the resulting samples with executable tools and structured large language model judges. Failed samples trigger diagnostic-guided recovery, while accepted samples are probed against the target model to estimate difficulty. The resulting verifier signals and target-model failure profiles are written back to memory, allowing subsequent rounds to adaptively expand the constraint space, reduce template repetition, and focus on unresolved model weaknesses. The same verifier contracts further provide reward signals for reinforcement learning without a separately trained reward model. Experiments on MM-IFEval show that VISA consistently improves multimodal instruction following over strong baselines, while preserving general multimodal capability across seven public benchmarks.

</details>


### [157] [Beyond Local Surprise: Grounded Dialogue as Selective Belief Revision under Referential Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26035)
*Ziming Liu,Bhanu Chaitanya Jasti,Ziyang Xu,Hongyu Wu,Yi Wu,Jiqun Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种数据驱动的对话决策框架，比较四种不同的听者理解修正策略，发现基于不确定性的策略能够更好地平衡理解连贯和信息检索效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言系统多以局部冲突为信号，快速调整对话理解，但实际对话中人们可能更保守，更倾向于在累积不确定性时才修改理解，动机在于更贴合对话实际和理论。

Method: 作者提出了一个可控的、数据驱动的对话回合决策框架，在相同条件下学习四种不同的听者理解修正策略，并对这些策略的表现进行比较和分析。

Result: （1）仅依靠局部冲突的修正策略反应灵敏，但会导致语境不稳定、信息检索能力下降；（2）基于不确定感知的修正策略结合了冲突信号和累积证据，既能保留连贯理解，又能保持强信息检索。

Conclusion: 人类听者在面对局部冲突时倾向于保留原有理解，只有当不确定性积累到一定程度才进行修正，这一策略更符合概念契约理论，有助于维持对话的连贯性和高效信息获取。

Abstract: When a speaker refers to a scene that the listener cannot directly see, the listener must decide whether to preserve its current understanding or revise it as new utterances arrive. Many language systems treat local mismatch as a cue for updating: divergence from the current understanding encourages adjustment. Yet conversational understanding may be more conservative, interpreting mismatching evidence relative to prior understanding rather than immediately revising it. We introduce a controlled, data-driven framework for turn-by-turn preserve/revise decisions in dialogue, where competing revision policies are learned under otherwise identical conditions. We compare four theory-driven revision strategies, each reflecting a different assumption about when listeners should preserve or revise. Two findings stand out. First, a mismatch-driven policy that updates solely based on local divergence reacts strongly to mismatch but destabilizes grounding and degrades retrieval. Second, an uncertainty-sensitive policy extends mismatch-based updating with accumulated evidence, preserving coherent understanding while maintaining strong retrieval performance. Surprisingly, coherent understanding emerges from a counterintuitive pattern: local mismatch promotes preservation, whereas accumulated uncertainty promotes revision, suggesting that listeners maintain prior understanding despite local mismatch and revise only when uncertainty sufficiently accumulates. This pattern is consistent with conceptual pact theory.

</details>


### [158] [Fine-Tuning Whisper for Automatic Speech Recognition in Baniwa: A Preliminary Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26060)
*Leonardo Duart,Tiago Fonseca,Thiago Chacón*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文尝试将Whisper模型用于极低资源的土著语言（巴尼瓦语）自动语音识别，通过微调模型取得了有意义的识别效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前ASR技术大多集中于高资源语言，土著语言如巴尼瓦语缺乏相应的语音资源和技术支持，导致信息鸿沟加剧。因此，研究如何将大型多语种模型适配到低资源语言具有重要意义。

Method: 作者采用收集到的1373条、总时长约0.54小时的巴尼瓦语录音（主要为孤立词和简短句子），在Whisper Small模型基础上，通过有监督微调，评估了ASR性能。

Result: 微调后的模型在测试集上取得了37.5%的单词错误率（WER）和7.45%的字符错误率（CER），证明多语种基础模型可迁移至极低资源语言。

Conclusion: 本研究初步建立了巴尼瓦语ASR的基线，为后续更大数据集、针对性迁移策略及后处理方法的研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) technologies have achieved remarkable performance in recent years through the use of large multilingual foundation models. However, most advances remain concentrated on high-resource languages, while indigenous languages continue to suffer from a lack of speech resources and language technologies. This work presents a preliminary study on the adaptation of Whisper for Automatic Speech Recognition in Baniwa, an indigenous Arawakan language spoken in Brazil, Colombia, and Venezuela. The experiments were conducted using a corpus of 1,373 manually transcribed recordings obtained from a linguistic documentation project. The corpus contains approximately 0.54 hours of speech and consists primarily of isolated words and short elicited utterances. The Whisper Small model was fine-tuned using supervised learning and evaluated using Word Error Rate (WER) and Character Error Rate (CER). The best model achieved a WER of 37.5% and a CER of 7.45%, demonstrating that multilingual foundation models can be successfully adapted to extremely low-resource indigenous languages. The results establish an initial baseline for Baniwa Automatic Speech Recognition and provide a foundation for future research involving larger datasets, language-specific adaptation strategies, and post-processing techniques.

</details>


### [159] [Prefix Sliding for efficient test-time scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26070)
*Niklas Muennighoff,Zhengyang Wang,Zeyi Chen,Weijia Shi,Binyuan Hui,John Yang,Dapeng Jiang,Mika Senghaas,Fares Obeid,Johannes Hagemann,Sami Jaghouar,Ludwig Schmidt,Percy Liang,Jason Wei,Andrew Y. Ng,Luke Zettlemoyer,Yejin Choi,Mike Lewis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Prefix Sliding的方法，通过有选择地丢弃部分推理过程中产生的中间token，提高大语言模型在推理时的效率和可扩展性，实现无需训练即可将推理速度提升3倍且保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型在推理时需保留完整的推理轨迹，占用大量内存，导致在需要长时间推理的复杂任务上效率极低，制约了推理能力的扩展。研究发现大部分中间token在推理后期作用逐渐减小，部分token的保留是不必要的。

Method: 提出Prefix Sliding方法：仅保留推理序列中的前缀token（关键信息或指令）以及最新的若干（几千）token，丢弃其他中间token。这样可将内存需求限制在固定范围内，无论推理持续多长时间。同时研究了无须训练的直接应用方式，以及基于强化学习训练以优化性能。

Result: 使用Prefix Sliding，无需重新训练即可让现有模型推理速度提升约3倍且几乎无损性能，经RL训练后还能提升对超长推理序列（10万token以上）的处理能力。消融实验显示，该方法优于对中间token进行摘要或直接滑动窗口方法。

Conclusion: Prefix Sliding解决了LLM推理时内存瓶颈问题，实现了高效的长序列推理和性能扩展。该方法简单实用，可直接用于现有模型，并有望推动语言模型在复杂任务中的应用。

Abstract: Test-time scaling uses extra test-time compute to improve performance, such as letting language models reason longer when solving a problem. As models keep the entire reasoning trace in memory via full attention, hard tasks that need long thinking can be prohibitively expensive. However, we find most intermediate reasoning tokens lose importance as the model continues reasoning. This calls into question whether retaining them is worth the cost. Based on this insight, we propose Prefix Sliding, which discards tokens during reasoning that are not part of the prefix or the window of the last few thousand tokens. The prefix has key instructions and tools available to the model, while the most recent tokens are the current reasoning the model is working on. This caps the total memory requirement regardless of how long the model reasons, allowing for efficient long-horizon test-time scaling. Without training, Prefix Sliding can make existing models 3x faster while maintaining performance. Training with Prefix Sliding using reinforcement learning can achieve better performance by enabling scaling to reasoning traces beyond a hundred thousand tokens. Ablations show Prefix Sliding outperforms summarizing intermediate tokens or vanilla sliding window. Our code is at https://github.com/Muennighoff/prefix-sliding

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [160] [GaussVLA: Geometry-Aware Spatial Reasoning for Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24959)
*Md Selim Sarowar,Md Tanvir Islam,Sungho Kim,Sangtae Ahn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GaussVLA模型，实现高效的视觉-语言-行动空间推理，在LIBERO数据集显著提升性能且参数量较少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前VLA模型缺乏对空间结构和几何的高效编码，难以进行高级空间推理，从而影响动作预测效果。

Method: 提出基于Mamba架构的GaussVLA，引入高斯空间标记器（GST）和深度感知链式推理模块（DA-CoT），通过这些模块将2D特征转换为3D高斯标记，并在语言与流动条件下进行非自回归的结构化空间推理。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中，GaussVLA表现出强大的空间操作能力。在LIBERO数据集上达到了93.5%的平均成功率，空间操作任务达到了100%的成功率，并且仅需2亿参数，在性能和参数效率上都优于SpatialVLA。

Conclusion: GaussVLA有效提升了VLA模型的空间推理与动作预测能力，为视觉-语言-动作任务提供了更结构化、高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models encode visual observations as flat 2D patch tokens that carry no intrinsic geometric structure, and augmenting them with dense monocular depth injects per-pixel scalar values that encode neither surface orientation nor geometric confidence. This leaves the policy with limited structured spatial reasoning for action prediction. We propose GaussVLA, a Mamba-based VLA that incorporates two custom modules: Gaussian Spatial Tokenizer (GST) to lift frozen semantic and depth features into compact 3D Gaussian tokens, pools geometrically salient regions with learned queries, and \emph{Depth-Aware Chain-of-Thought (DA-CoT)} that performs structured, non-autoregressive geometric reasoning under language and flow-time conditioning. Across both simulation and real-world evaluations, GaussVLA demonstrates strong spatial-manipulation performance while remaining parameter-efficient. On LIBERO, it achieves 93.5% average success and 100.0% success on the Spatial suite with only 200M parameters, improving over SpatialVLA by 19.7% relative average success while remaining significantly more parameter-efficient.

</details>


### [161] [ROS2 Connect: A new ROS2 over WAN Solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25102)
*Daniel Schott,Lakshminarasimhan Srinivasan,Christian Herrmann,Andreas Nüchter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了ROS2 Connect，一种基于WebSocket的通信框架，使ROS2能在广域网（WAN）环境下安全、无需更改网络基础设施地进行分布式机器人交互，并通过实验证明其性能优于现有方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前ROS2在广域网环境下部署分布式机器人系统受到限制，原因是其底层DDS/RTPS通信机制需依赖不可用的组播发现功能，导致远程操控与分布式协作困难。作者希望解决这一实际应用瓶颈。

Method: 作者设计了一种基于WebSocket的客户端-服务器通信架构，可透明转发ROS2中的话题、服务、动作与系统数据，并内嵌了认证和访问控制机制，无需更改网络与DDS配置。

Result: 在真实WAN网络环境下，ROS2 Connect实现了比DDS Router、rosbridge和Zenoh更低的延迟、更高的稳定性和更好的可扩展性。实验表明其显著优于现有解决方案。

Conclusion: ROS2 Connect为ROS2系统在广域网上的远程操控与分布式应用提供了可靠基础，有望推动机器人系统在更大范围的实际部署。

Abstract: The Robot Operating System 2 (ROS2) has become a widely adopted framework for the development of distributed robotic systems. However, its communication architecture, based on DDS and RTPS, relies on multicast discovery mechanisms that are typically unavailable in wide-area network (WAN) environments, making remote operation challenging. This work presents ROS2 Connect, a WebSocket-based communication framework that enables transparent and secure ROS2 interaction across routed networks without requiring modifications to network infrastructure or DDS configurations. The proposed client-server architecture supports bidirectional exchange of topics, services, actions, and system data while integrating authentication and access control mechanisms. Experimental evaluation over a real WAN connection demonstrates significantly lower latency, higher stability, and improved scalability compared to existing solutions, including DDS Router, rosbridge and Zenoh. Initial results show that ROS2 Connect provides a reliable foundation for teleoperation and distributed robotics applications over wide-area networks.

</details>


### [162] [Extending Ground-Constraint LiDAR-IMU Calibration to Tilted Surfaces in a Continuous-Time Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25135)
*Vassili Korotkine,Pierre Chamoun,Mohammed Ayman Shalaby,James Richard Forbes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需靶标的LiDAR-IMU标定新方法，可适用于非平坦地面环境，解决了现有方法只适用于平坦地面的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地面车辆的LiDAR-IMU标定方法需要传感器系统的完全激励，而普通地面车辆难以满足这一条件，且主流方法假设地面平坦、重力方向与表面法线共线，限制了应用场景。

Method: 提出了一种新的地面残差模型，在标定过程中不再要求地面平坦，可以处理倾斜面上的平面运动，实现了更通用的标定。方法在多个数据集上进行验证，并开源了实现代码。

Result: 实验结果表明，在倾斜和水平地面场景下，该方法均提高了标定算法的重复性，特别是在倾斜地面情况下有显著提升。

Conclusion: 新方法突破了以往仅能用于平坦地面的局限，使LiDAR-IMU标定能在更多实际地形中有效应用，对机器人地面定位导航具有实际意义。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel method that extends targetless LiDAR-IMU calibration for ground vehicles to non- flat environments. Calibration typically necessitates full exci- tation of the sensor rig, a requirement that is not fulfilled by ground vehicles in normal operation. To address the degenerate planar motion, state-of-the-art methods propose residuals that assume the colinearity of the gravity and physical surface normal vectors, restricting usage to cases where the ground is assumed flat. This paper proposes ground-plane residuals that do not require this assumption, and are applicable for planar motion on a tilted surface. Results are demonstrated on a dataset collected from a Husky ground vehicle, on the M2DGR dataset, as well as on an offroad vehicle dataset. Repeatability is shown to be improved both in tilted and flat-ground scenarios, with strong improvement demonstrated for the tilted case. The implementation and experiments are open-sourced at https://github.com/vkorotkine/licalib_tilted_ground.

</details>


### [163] [SkyDrive: Learning to Drive in a New City from Aerial Traffic Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25142)
*Weijiang Xiong,Lan Feng,Alexandre Alahi,Nikolas Geroliminis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出利用无人机航拍监控获得道路交通数据，以更高效地适配自动驾驶系统到新城市，减少对本地车辆数据采集的依赖。通过137小时航拍获得65万条驾驶样本，零样本实验显示少量无人机监督即可大幅缓解跨城市域移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶系统在新城市环境下零样本迁移时性能下降明显，主要源于交通规则、道路布局和驾驶行为的差异。传统适配方法需要大量本地数据采集，过程昂贵且耗时，迫切需要更高效、可扩展的数据采集与监督方式。

Method: 作者提出了SkyDrive框架，利用无人机进行空中交通监控，从航拍视频中提取多车辆驾驶行为样本，制作大规模轨迹预测与规划数据集。相比传统车载采集方式，航拍能同时获取多个车辆长期轨迹，提高数据采集效率和覆盖范围。通过多模型跨城市零样本实验评估无人机监督效果。

Result: 从137小时无人机监控视频中提取了65万条驾驶样本，建立了可用于轨迹规划和运动预测的基准数据集。实验发现，传统模型在跨城市适配时性能明显下降，但通过每个新地点仅30分钟的无人机数据采集，即可显著提升模型适应能力，缩小域间性能差距。

Conclusion: 无人机航拍监控为自动驾驶系统在新环境的快速适配提供了高效、可扩展的数据采集方案，显著减少了依赖本地车载传感器采集的资源消耗。实验表明，该方法有效缓解了跨域迁移带来的性能衰减，未来有望推动自动驾驶系统落地新城市。

Abstract: Autonomous driving has made remarkable progress through imitation learning with massive human demonstration data. However, a trained planner often degrades severely when applied to a new environment zero-shot, because of domain shifts in traffic regulations, road layout and driving behaviors. Therefore, adapting a trajectory planner to a new city typically requires resource-demanding local data collection with a vehicle sensor suite. In this work, we show that driving behavior can be learned from a scalable and efficient alternative. We introduce \emph{SkyDrive}, a framework that utilizes drone-based traffic monitoring to provide efficient supervision for autonomous driving agents in a new environment. While vehicle-based data collection logs the ego and its surroundings, an aerial platform naturally observes many road users simultaneously over an extended field of view. As a result, every vehicle can be a data source with grounded driving behavior, effectively scaling up the amount of supervision. Based on 137 hours of aerial traffic monitoring footage, we extract 650K driving samples and construct a benchmark for trajectory planners and motion predictors. Zero-shot experiments with multiple models reveal significant cross-city domain gaps, but many of them can be alleviated by limited supervision from the sky, e.g., 30 minutes of monitoring per location. Our findings show that aerial traffic monitoring is an efficient and scalable data source for adapting autonomous driving systems in new cities. Data and code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [164] [Sequential Object Placement Optimization with Convex Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25162)
*Yuezhe Zhang,Xiangyu Lyu,Sohan Rudra,Davide Tateo,Georgia Chalvatzaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效的机器人异形物体装箱方法，突破了以往在空间离散化和计算效率上的限制，实现毫秒级装箱速度，并在多种任务和真实硬件上验证了其实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统装箱方法在处理连续空间高精度装箱和不规则物体时，计算效率极低，且难以应对实际场景中的动力学和接触约束。亟需一种无需细粒度离散化、效率高且通用性强的新方法。

Method: 作者提出了SOPO-CD顺序优化框架，将物体放置建模为分解自由空间中的可微非线性优化问题。证明了凸物体放入凸包时等价于顶点受约束，约束及其导数可闭式表达，并实现了超快自定义求解器。该方法拓展到2D和3D多种装箱场景，并在仿真及真实机器人上测试。

Result: 自定义求解器实现了在紧凑空间中毫秒级（比经典网格搜索快100倍）最优放置。该框架在Tangram、Tetris和3D装箱等任务上均展现了优异性能，并通过Allegro Hand与Xarm在线完成Tangram拼图，验证了其实用性。

Conclusion: SOPO-CD框架打破了离散化的计算瓶颈，实现了高精度、高效率和通用性的机器人装箱方案，对物流等场景的自动化部署具有重要意义。

Abstract: Robotic object packing has been a core challenge for robotic deployment in logistics, industry, etc., due to the curse of dimensionality in combinatorial search and the difficulty of dealing with dynamic and contact constraints for irregularly shaped objects. Current heuristic and learning-based methods assume a limited spatial discretization resolution of space, and computation becomes extremely inefficient as discretization accuracy increases. In this work, we eliminate these assumptions by introducing SOPO-CD, a sequential optimization framework that frames object placement as a differentiable nonlinear optimization problem in a decomposed free space. We prove that placing a convex object inside a convex hull is essentially constraining the vertices of the object inside the convex hull. The constraints and their derivatives can be written in closed form and calculated within $200$ns. We implement a custom solver that achieves optimal placement within tightly constrained space in milliseconds; a $100 \times$ speedup compared to a classical grid search method. We generalize our framework to 2D Tangram, 2D Tetris, and 3D Bin Packing, and have demonstrated strong computational performance and packing utility. We also demonstrate solving a real-world Tangram puzzle online using an Allegro Hand and an Xarm.

</details>


### [165] [Control-Oriented Learning for Dynamic Tracking and Stability Analysis of Soft Pneumatic Actuators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25171)
*Nithin S. Kumar,Eric J. Barth*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合数据驱动建模与控制的新框架来高效控制软气动执行器，能实现高精度轨迹跟踪和实时避障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软气动执行器具有结构柔顺和安全性高的优点，但由于高度非线性和分布式动态，难以精确建模与控制，亟需新方法提升其实用性。

Method: 将执行器的行为分解为非线性的静态平衡模型与线性残差动态模型，并利用带控制项的扩展动态模态分解（EDMDc）进行系统辨识，构建便于控制的线性增广模型，并支持前馈补偿、任务空间反馈控制及局部闭环稳定性分析。

Result: 实验中，低速轨迹跟踪（约10mm/s）时均方根误差约1mm，高速（约100mm/s）时低于10mm。还可稳定跟踪高动态用户输入（峰值加速度超25m/s^2），并实现了实时避障。稳定性分析也能准确预测不同操作状态。

Conclusion: 该结构化、面向控制的学习框架能提供准确、实用的软执行器控制方法，为工程应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Soft pneumatic actuators offer inherent compliance and safe interaction but remain difficult to model and control because of their highly nonlinear, distributed dynamics. We present a control-oriented data-driven modeling and control framework that decomposes actuator behavior into a nonlinear static equilibrium model and a linear residual dynamics model identified using Extended Dynamic Mode Decomposition with control (EDMDc). This representation enables feedforward compensation, task-space feedback control, and local closed-loop stability analysis through an augmented linear model. Experiments achieve approximately 1 mm root mean square error (RMSE) during low-speed (approximately 10 mm/s) trajectory tracking and below 10 mm RMSE at higher speeds (approximately 100 mm/s). The framework further achieves stable tracking of highly dynamic user-generated references with peak accelerations exceeding 25 m/s^2 while simultaneously performing real-time obstacle avoidance. Finally, the proposed stability analysis is experimentally validated by accurately predicting stable, marginal, and unstable operating regimes. These results demonstrate that structured, control-oriented learning provides an accurate and practical framework for soft actuator control.

</details>


### [166] [CRESSim-Neo: A Batched GPU Simulation Engine for Surgical Robotics and Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25192)
*Yafei Ou,Ahnaf Naheen,Tleukhan Mussin,Hans Jarales,Melwin Moncy,Mahdi Tavakoli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了CRESSim-Neo，一款面向手术机器人和机器人学习的GPU批量仿真引擎，能够高效模拟刚体、可变形体、流体等多种复杂环境，实现大规模并行和高效的数据生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有外科手术和机器学习仿真引擎在性能、统一性和可扩展性上存在不足，难以支持复杂多样的医学模拟场景。因此需要一个高效、统一，并能无缝集成现代深度学习框架的仿真平台。

Method: CRESSim-Neo将基于位置的物理仿真与GPU批量渲染结合，支持刚体、可变形组织、流体、线状物的高效模拟，并提供面向手术的传感和无缝的数据流。通过直接访问GPU端的物理和渲染缓冲区，实现与PyTorch等深度学习库的零拷贝集成（DLPack）。

Result: CRESSim-Neo在NVIDIA RTX 4090上表现优异，可在8192个并行CartPole环境中达到每秒203万仿真步，并可扩展到涉及组织变形、流体交互和超声成像等复杂手术场景。

Conclusion: CRESSim-Neo提供了一个高效统一且可扩展的平台，适用于手术仿真、合成数据生成与手术机器人智能的研究与开发。

Abstract: We introduce CRESSim-Neo, a batched GPU simulation engine for surgical robotics and robot learning. CRESSim-Neo combines position-based simulation of rigid bodies, deformable tissues, fluids, and strands with batched rendering, surgery-specific sensing, and a GPU-resident data pipeline. The engine supports applications including tissue manipulation, fluid suction, suturing, cable-driven robots, and ultrasound image synthesis. Direct access to physics and rendering buffers enables GPU-resident robot learning and zero-copy PyTorch integration using DLPack. We demonstrate CRESSim-Neo across rigid-body, deformable-body, and fluid simulation tasks, including vision-based and surgical robot-learning scenarios. On an NVIDIA RTX 4090, the engine achieves up to 2.03 million environment steps per second for 8192 parallel CartPole environments, and scales to batched surgical scenarios involving tissue deformation, fluid interaction, and ultrasound sensing. Overall, CRESSim-Neo provides a unified and scalable platform for surgical simulation, synthetic data generation, and surgical robot learning.

</details>


### [167] [Longitudinal Robot Learning from Demonstration with Care Providers in a Home Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25196)
*Nina Moorman,Julianna Schalkwyk,Vriksha Srihari,Qingyu Xiao,Kamel Alrashedy,Hongseok Jeong,Kiersten Lange,Matthew B. Luebbers,Matthew Gombolay*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了非专业用户在家中无机器人专家指导的情况下，通过示范教学机器人遇到的主要障碍，并引入了两种支持措施进行实验。提出将相关数据集开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LfD（从示范中学习）方法多在实验室中与专家干预下评估，缺乏在真实家庭环境中、普通终端用户自主教学的相关调研和数据。作者旨在填补这一实际使用场景下对非专家用户体验与障碍的认知空白。

Method: 通过人类受试者实验，为非专家终端用户提供两种基于以往工作的示范指导：预训练和自适应反馈。实验对象为家庭护理人员，多次上门评估用户在无专家的真实家庭环境中教学机器人的实际情况且收集数据。

Result: 实验证明，预训练和自适应反馈可以在一定程度上支持非专家用户完成基于示范的机器人教学，有效识别和分析了用户面临的主要障碍。

Conclusion: 评价了LfD方法在实际家庭环境中的可用性，为今后改进人机共学体验提供了依据。同时开源实验数据集，有助于推动该研究领域的发展。

Abstract: Learning from demonstration (LfD) methods enable non-expert end users to teach robots novel skills without explicit programming. However most evaluations of the usability of LfD with non-experts has been conducted in controlled laboratory environments with a robotics experimenter present. In this work we identify non-expert end users' key barriers when teaching robots via demonstration without live robotics expert feedback in a home environment. In our human subjects experiment we support the non-expert end users through two forms of demonstrator guidance developed in prior work: pre-training and adaptive feedback. Towards the ecological validity of the evaluation, we conduct this experimentation over multiple visits, with a population of care providers. Finally, we propose to open source the resulting LfD dataset of care providers teaching a robot assistive tasks over multiple visits to a home environment.

</details>


### [168] [Development of a Voice-Controlled Tendon-Driven Bionic Hand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25222)
*Urja Kohli,Shagata Chanda,Kritika Gandhi,Charu Nigam,Aditi Surya Kamal,Pooja Bhati*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文介绍了一种基于肌腱驱动的自动仿生手设计，具备声控和高效动作控制，实现了稳定的手指协调与抓握。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手部功能障碍会严重影响个体日常生活能力，因此开发稳定且可控的辅助设备具有重要意义。

Method: 采用肌腱驱动机构与伺服电机实现手指运动，基于Arduino微控制器进行手势控制，通过蓝牙支持的语音命令接口进行交互。

Result: 实验表明，系统可在7-8秒内完成全手指弯曲，动作精确重复，能稳定抓取不同形状和尺寸的物体，具备良好协调性。

Conclusion: 所提系统结构简单高效，动作控制表现可预测且稳定，适合未来辅助仿生手发展。

Abstract: The impairment of the hands can seriously affect the abilities of every individual to perform the every-day activity, so the design of stable and controllable support devices is a significant field of study. This paper is about the design and implementation of an automated bionic hand which is dedicated to the coordinated finger movement through the simplified and efficient actuation mechanism. The method that the proposed system was designed on is the tendon-based method whereby the servo motors generate the movement of the fingers, with assistance of the angular control which is calibrated. An actuation is controlled by a microcontroller that will be programmed by use of an Arduino-based microcontroller to carry out programmed gestures that include open hand, fist, pinch and half flexion. It has an interface that is voice command enabled to make it easy to interact with a Bluetooth based sender receiver architecture which offers an option of executing trained commands which are immediately converted to finger actions. To explore the motions behavior, finger coordination and control response to the input, the behavior of the experiment system is tested. The actuation of the fingers was found to take a total of about 7-8 seconds to achieve full flexion of all fingers in a sequence. The system showed repetitive and constant motion throughout several actuation cycles without loss of any apparent tension or precision of control. There was a stable grasp of objects of different shapes and sizes, which implied consistent coordination between the fingers. These findings indicate that the proposed system offers predictable and steady control behavior and has a simple and efficient mechanical and control architecture.

</details>


### [169] [Generative Action-Chunk Sampling for Adaptive Stiffness Control in Physical Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25284)
*Aoi Otake,Ferdinand Hartmann,Ko Igari,Shingo Murata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于生成式动作片段采样的自适应刚度框架，实现了在物理人机协作中兼顾辅助和服从，取得了显著的实验效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理人机协作要求机器人在理解人类意图明确时提供帮助，而在人类意图不确定时则需保持柔顺，避免过度干预。传统刚度固定的方法难以兼顾这两点，因此需要一种能够动态调整刚度的策略。

Method: 该方法基于生成式模型，利用RGB图像和外部关节力矩估计，实时采样多个未来的动作片段。通过分析这些采样动作之间的变异性来自动调整机器人各关节的刚度和阻尼：采样动作差异大时（表示人类意图不明确），提升柔顺性；差异小时则增强辅助力度。

Result: 在一个实际协作搬运任务（有四个可能方向）中，该方法实现了0.95的平均成功率，明显优于固定刚度（0.83）和确定性基线（0.69）。在人类意图尚未明确时，系统成功识别这种不确定性并降低刚度。

Conclusion: 采样动作的变异性可以作为一种在线控制信号，实现人机协作中的自适应辅助与柔顺。在实际任务中，所提框架优于传统固定参数方法，有效平衡了助力与服从。

Abstract: Physical human-robot collaboration requires a robot to provide assistance when human intention is clear while remaining compliant when several future motions are plausible. We present an adaptive stiffness framework based on generative action-chunk sampling. Conditioned on an RGB image and external joint-torque estimates, the policy samples multiple future action chunks from an observation-conditioned prior. Variation among the sampled action chunks is used to continuously adapt joint stiffness and damping. Greater variation makes the robot more compliant to facilitate human guidance, whereas lower variation provides firmer assistance. In a real-world collaborative transport task with four possible directions, the proposed method achieved an average success rate of 0.95, compared with 0.83 for a fixed-stiffness ablation and 0.69 for a deterministic baseline. Near direction determination, variation among the sampled action chunks increased and the controller accordingly reduced stiffness. These results suggest that variation among actions sampled by a generative policy can serve as an online control signal for balancing assistance and compliance in physical human-robot interaction.

</details>


### [170] [RAEM: Robust Autonomous Exploration for Multi-Floor Environments with a Quadruped Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25366)
*Zikang Yuan,Yuan Ren,Yian Wang,Yixue Wang,Enze Fang,Xuewei Zhang,Junda Cheng,Chi Chen,Chin-Pang Ho,Lijun Zhu,Shaohang Xu,Kwang-Ting Cheng,Xin Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了RAEM框架，使四足机器人在多楼层环境中实现稳定自动探索，克服了多层建筑导航中的地图构建与路径规划难点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地面机器人探索方法通常依赖于平面可通行性表示，难以处理多楼层建筑的重叠结构与跨层连接问题。全局断层成像虽能建模多层环境，但计算量大，不适合在线探索。此外，楼梯区域激光雷达数据稀疏，影响局部可通行性估计，导致导航断连。

Method: RAEM采用混合局部-全局可通行性建模：局部采用断层成像图及显式3D网格进行地形分析与连通性评估，全局逐步构建考虑高度的拓扑图进行跨层路径规划。引入楼梯中心对齐和双路径搜索机制，分别提升上楼梯时姿态稳定性和局部拓扑断裂时的路径恢复能力。

Result: 通过大量仿真和实地实验，RAEM在包括连续穿越五层楼梯在内的多楼层场景下展现出良好的自主探索能力与计算稳定性。

Conclusion: RAEM显著提升四足机器人在复杂多楼层建筑中的自主探索稳定性和效率，兼顾局部地形分析与全球路径规划，为多层场景下的机器人探索任务提供有效解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose RAEM, a robust autonomous exploration framework for quadruped robots operating in multi-floor environments. Most existing ground-robot exploration approaches rely on planar traversability representations, which cannot adequately represent the overlapping structures and cross-floor connectivity of multi-floor buildings. Although tomography-based representations provide effective traversability modeling for multi-floor navigation, maintaining a global tomography map incurs substantial computational overhead for online exploration with frequent replanning. Moreover, sparse and fragmented LiDAR observations in stairwells can degrade local traversability estimation, leading to irregular viewpoint placement and temporary topological disconnections. To address these challenges, RAEM adopts a hybrid local-global traversability representation, in which a local tomography map and an explicitly categorized local 3D grid map are used for online terrain analysis and connectivity evaluation, while an elevation-aware global topological graph is incrementally constructed from these local spatial representations for efficient cross-floor exploration planning. We further introduce a staircase center alignment strategy to reduce abrupt yaw variations during climbing and a dual path searching mechanism to recover guidance paths when the global topology is locally disconnected. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate robust and computationally stable autonomous exploration across multi-floor structures, including continuous exploration of a five-floor stairwell.

</details>


### [171] [A Taxonomy of Construction Task Activities for Robot Workers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25395)
*Sadman Sakib,Zhangyi None Peng,Yujie Pang,Yu Otsuki,Mohammad Abdullah Al Faruque*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出了TARCAT，一种面向建筑业的动作原语分类体系，可为机器人开发提供通用技能标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉-语言-动作模型虽能扩展机器人的任务能力，但在建筑部署中需细致了解人类工种活动及机器人所需能力，业界缺乏统一的动作原语分类与技能组织方法。

Method: 通过分析91个O*NET任务（涵盖七个建筑高就业工种）及30段实际工作视频，作者归纳出41个动作原语，分属12组、3类，并设计了原语参数化组合成可复用技能的机制，对人类活动进行有解释性的结构化描述。

Result: 以DOBOT CR3机械臂及CRAFT机械手实现部分动作原语示范，证明该体系可指导机器人执行建筑任务，并可以支撑技能库检索和扩展。

Conclusion: TARCAT为分析人类工作及开发通用建筑机器人提供了共通的动作词汇和分类结构，有助于标准化演示、明确机器人技能需求和推进智能体技术发展。相关标注数据已开源。

Abstract: Recent vision-language-action models offer a path toward robots with broader repertoires than conventional task-specific systems. Construction deployment, however, requires a precise inventory of worker activities and the capabilities needed to execute them. We present TARCAT, an occupation-grounded taxonomy derived from 91 O*NET tasks across seven high-employment construction occupations and 30 instructional videos of physical work. TARCAT defines 41 action primitives in 12 groups and three classes and provides a mechanism for composing parameterized primitive sequences into reusable skills. This human-interpretable structure can organize demonstrations, specify robot requirements, and support coding agents that retrieve and extend skill libraries. We also demonstrate selected primitives on a DOBOT CR3 arm with a CRAFT hand. TARCAT thereby provides a common vocabulary for analyzing human work and developing general-purpose construction robots. Annotations are available at https://github.com/AICPS/TARCAT-Taxonomy.

</details>


### [172] [LAC: Linear and Angular Compliance for Humanoid Whole-body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25405)
*Yang Liu,Zhongkai Gu,Wei Zhu,Mitsuhiro Hayashibe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种用于人形机器人上半身的全身线性与角向柔顺控制器LAC，并通过大量仿真与实验证实其在实际环境中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有控制器要么将外力视为扰动排除，要么仅实现身体部分的有限柔顺，并忽视角向效应，远不能满足现实世界中与人和物体物理交互的复杂需求。

Method: 作者提出线性与角向柔顺控制器（LAC），首先通过人机交互数据采集接触帧，采样外力与力偶生成大规模增强数据集；然后在每个接触链路上实施外力和虚拟扭矩，基于设定刚度实现虚拟从动性；随后采用教师-学生强化学习训练单一策略，以跟踪受外部扳手影响下的柔顺运动。

Result: 大量仿真与现实实验显示，该方法能在上半身范围内实现对外部扳手的全身柔顺响应，并能在全刚度调节范围内实现单调调制，适用远程操作下的人形机器人移动操控任务。

Conclusion: LAC控制器实现了人形机器人全身的线性与角向柔顺控制，突破了现有方法的局限，显著提升了机器人面对实际物理交互任务的能力。

Abstract: Real-world humanoid tasks involve physical interaction with objects and humans, yet current controllers either reject external forces as disturbances or restrict compliance to limited body links while ignoring angular effects. We present LAC, a general whole-body controller that simultaneously realizes commanded Linear and Angular Compliance for wrenches applied to the upper body. First, we synthesize whole-body compliant responses into a large-scale augmented dataset. Sampled force and couple events are imposed on contact frames extracted from human interaction data. At each contact link, the external force and a virtual torque from the passively yielding kinematic chain drive a virtual admittance under the commanded stiffness. Subsequently, teacher-student reinforcement learning trains a single policy to track the compliant motions under external wrenches. Finally, extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate whole-body compliant responses to wrenches across the upper body, monotonic modulation over the full range of both stiffness commands, and applicability to teleoperated loco-manipulation tasks. Project website: https://lac-humanoid.github.io/

</details>


### [173] [SUPER ODOMETRY 2.0: Resilient Odometry via Hierarchical Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25427)
*Shibo Zhao,Sifan Zhou,Yuchen Zhang,Ji Zhang,Chen Wang,Wenshan Wang,Sebastian Scherer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Super Odometry的全新传感器融合系统，通过自适应融合IMU、相机和激光雷达，实现机器人在各种极端和退化环境下的可靠定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有里程计方案在烟雾、风沙、低光等极端环境中性能大幅下降，严重威胁自主机器人的安全与功能，迫切需要在多种传感器退化条件下依然鲁棒的定位方法。

Method: Super Odometry采用层级式结构，集成了自适应特征选择、自适应状态方向选择、自适应引擎选择及新型学习驱动惯导模块（使用超百小时多平台异构数据训练），动态应对不同程度环境退化，并提升IMU在定位系统中的地位。

Result: 该方法在200公里、800小时的多种机器人平台（包括空中、轮式和足式）上进行测试，能在多种传感器组合、恶劣环境及剧烈运动中稳定工作。

Conclusion: Super Odometry为机器人在全退化极端环境下的安全与长期自主运行提供了关键技术，为未来自主系统落地奠定基础。

Abstract: Resilient and robust odometry is crucial for autonomous systems operating in complex and dynamic environments. Existing odometry systems often struggle with severe sensory degradations and extreme conditions such as smoke, sandstorms, snow, or low-light conditions, threatening both the safety and functionality of robots. To address these challenges, we present Super Odometry, a sensor fusion framework that dynamically adapts to varying levels of environmental degradation. Super Odometry employs a hierarchical structure to integrate four core modules from lower-level to higher-level adaptability including adaptive feature selection, adaptive state direction selection, adaptive engine selection, and a novel learning- based inertial odometry. The inertial odometry, trained on over 100 hours of heterogeneous robotic platforms, captures comprehensive motion dynamics. Super Odometry elevates the inertial measurement unit (IMU) to equal importance with camera and LiDAR within the sensor fusion framework, providing a reliable fallback when exteroceptive sensors fail. Super Odometry has been validated across 200 kilometers and 800 operational hours on a fleet of aerial, wheeled, and legged robots, under diverse sensor configurations, environmental degradation, and aggressive motion profiles. It marks an important step towards safe and long-term robotic autonomy in all-degraded environments.

</details>


### [174] [Towards safe and optimal flight: Viability Kernel MPC for Fully Actuated Multirotor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25459)
*Massimiliano Bertoni,Alberto Piccina,Gianni Lunardi,Elias Fontanari,Andrea Del Prete,Angelo Cenedese,Giulia Michieletto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种在MPC框架下，为全驱动多旋翼无人机生成安全位姿轨迹的方法，并通过数值仿真验证了其实时与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业无人机在非结构化环境中导航时，需要兼顾安全性、性能以及计算效率，但现有方法常难以有效兼顾这些需求。

Method: 利用可行性理论和数据驱动方法，在MPC中动态生成轴对齐包围盒实现障碍物规避，无需离线可达性分析即可提供安全保障。

Result: 通过对倾斜六旋翼无人机的数值仿真，验证了该方法可在复杂环境中实时导航并保障安全。

Conclusion: 所提方法能高效保障全驱动多旋翼无人机在复杂环境中的安全导航，具有较强实用性与实时性。

Abstract: Industrial aerial robotics demands safety guarantees for navigation in unstructured environments while optimizing performance and computational efficiency. This paper presents a method for generating safe pose trajectories for fully actuated multirotors within a Model Predictive Control (MPC) framework, leveraging both viability theory and data-driven methods. Obstacle avoidance is enforced through dynamically computed axis-aligned bounding boxes, providing formal safety guarantees without exhaustive offline reachability analysis. Numerical simulations on a fully actuated tilted hexarotor validate the approach, demonstrating successful navigation in cluttered environments with real-time computational performance.

</details>


### [175] [Transient multimode heat transfer of an industrial automated tape laying process under rapidly changing conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25470)
*Bernhard Rameder,Hubert Gattringer,Andreas Müller,Ronald Naderer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种工业自动化铺带（ATL）过程中的瞬态传热模型，改进了传统铺带热模型的准确性，精准模拟热传输过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统复合材料制造中的热模型在精度上存在不足，特别是在辐射热流估计和对流效应的处理上存在系统性偏差，因此需要开发更高保真度的热模型以提升ATL工艺质量。

Method: 论文建立了一个耦合对流、传导、辐射等多热传递机制的热传导方程模型。采用解析视向因子的创新方法，考虑有限宽度的发射源和胶带，修正了1.5D模型对辐射热流的高估问题；同时，局部混合对流用Richardson数表征。整个系统被建模为带状基体与红外热源相互作用，胶带用双节点模型离散，采用高阶隐式积分保证数值稳定。

Result: 模型在工业ATL生产线上进行了实验验证，在大范围速度和电流扰动下，预测误差仅为1.08%（NRMSE），精度较高。

Conclusion: 新模型为ATL工艺的热状态精确估算和过程优化奠定了物理基础，有助于在制过程中实现一致的固化和更高的零件质量。

Abstract: This work presents a transient heat-transfer model of an industrial automated tape laying (ATL) process designed to overcome the limitations of conventional thermal models in composite manufacturing. The model solves the heat-conduction equation with coupled advection, conduction, convection, and radiation. A key innovation is the implementation of an analytical view factor approach that accounts for finite emitter and tape widths, thereby correcting systematic overestimations of radiative heat flux inherent in 1.5D simplifications. Furthermore, a local convection assessment incorporates mixed convection effects characterized by the Richardson number, ensuring accuracy across a wide range of process speeds. The ATL system is represented by two interacting subsystems: the moving tape substrate and the infrared heat sources. The tape is discretized using a two-node model that resolves the physical phase shift between the heated and monitored surfaces. Numerical stability under high dynamics is ensured by a monolithic solution strategy using a high-order implicit integration scheme. Model predictions were validated on an industrial ATL line, demonstrating an overall deviation of only 1.08% (NRMSE) under rapid velocity and current modulations. This framework provides a high-fidelity, physics-based foundation for thermal state estimation, supporting consistent in-situ consolidation and improved part quality.

</details>


### [176] [Dynamic Modeling of a Welding Torch Umbilical and Its Impact on Robot Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25509)
*Nicolas Gautier,Yves Guillermit,Mathieu Porez,Fabien Rousset,Damien Chablat*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于分析焊接电缆（脐带）动力学影响的多体动力学建模方法，并通过案例验证，提升了对机器人物理运动影响的建模准确性与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着轻量化与协作机器人在工业焊接中的应用增加，连接焊炬与发电机的脐带对机器人运动及驱动力的动态影响变得关键。此前此类影响常被忽略或简化，但在实际高精度应用中却不可忽略。

Method: 将焊接脐带建模为由被动关节相连的串联刚体链，并引入弹性与耗散效应。通过完整的运动学约束，将动力学方程投影到可行速度子空间，从而避免了拉格朗日乘子的使用，使模型更高效。

Result: 该方法能够高效、准确地模拟脐带对机器人的反作用力（wrench），并通过平面案例验证了建模与计算的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的约束多体动力学模型为分析机器人焊接脐带影响提供了高效且实用的工具，可为设计与控制提供理论与工程支持，尤其适合新型协作机器人场景。

Abstract: Robotic welding is widely used in industrial manufacturing, where the welding torch is often connected to the generator through an external umbilical. With the increasing deployment of lightweight and collaborative robots, the dynamic influence of this umbilical can significantly affect the robot motion and the actuation forces. This paper proposes a constrained multibody dynamic model of a welding umbilical, represented as a serial chain of rigid bodies interconnected by passive joints with elastic and dissipative effects. Prescribed motions at the distal anchor point are introduced through holonomic kinematic constraints. The equations of motion are reduced by projecting the dynamics onto the subspace of admissible velocities, yielding an efficient formulation free of Lagrange multipliers. The reaction wrench exerted by the umbilical on the robot is explicitly recovered. A planar case study illustrates the approach.

</details>


### [177] [A Tendon-Driven Five-Fingered Hand with Distributed Tactile Perception for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25547)
*Huayang Chen,Longhui Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型腱驱动五指机械手，并集成了分布式双模态触觉感知系统，提升了机械手的灵巧性和触觉感知能力，实验证明该系统具备优异的操作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧的机械手和高性能触觉感知能力是实现复杂操作的类人机器人所必需，目前这些方面仍有限制，因此需要新的设计来提升机械手的操控性和感知能力。

Method: 设计了一种软刚混合结构的腱驱动五指机械手，将双模态触觉传感元件分布在每个手指的远端和中部指骨，实现静态接触和动态力变化的同时感知。

Result: 通过数数手势、指尖对捏、物体抓取和瓶子抓持等实验，验证了该机械手集成驱动与感知系统的可行性和良好性能。

Conclusion: 该腱驱动五指机械手在提升灵巧性和触觉感知能力方面具有显著优势，为类人机器人复杂操作和人机交互提供了新途径。

Abstract: To apply the techniques of embodied artificial intelligence to human-oid robots for complex manipulations, dexterous robotic hands are indispensable, which are restricted by the dexterity and tactile perception capability. In this work, we proposed a novel design of tendon-driven five-fingered hand with dis-tributed tactile perception. With a soft-rigid-hybrid structure employed, both compliance and operational force are endowed to the hand. Dual-modality tactile sensing elements are distributed on the distal and middle phalanges of all five fingers, enabling the simultaneous detection of static contact and dynamic force variations. Manipulation experiments, including counting gestures, finger-to-thumb pinching, object grasping, and bottle-grasp tactile recording, demonstrate the feasibility of the integrated actuation-perception system.

</details>


### [178] [ConfAL-WM: Confidence-Guided Active Learning for Action-Conditioned World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25572)
*Xiang Liu,Sen Cui,Changshui Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于置信度引导的主动学习框架（ConfAL-WM），可用于提升具身世界模型在新任务和场景分布下的泛化能力，尤其在机器臂、接触区域及遮挡区域等局部空间表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身世界模型在机器人等领域有广泛应用，但当模型用于新任务和场景时，错误往往集中在时空局部区域（如机器人手臂、被操作物体、接触和遮挡部位），严重影响预测和规划能力。因此，如何高效地识别并提升这些局部区域下的模型性能是重要挑战。

Method: 作者提出ConfAL-WM方法：1）为UNet解码器特征附加轻量级置信度探针，预测潜空间的密集置信度图；2）将置信度汇聚为任务、帧和局部patch级别的分数，实现高效的数据选择和局部训练增强。框架流程包括：利用少量目标域数据热身置信度探针与主模型、任务级预筛选分配采样预算、基于选择数据进行重训练并可选帧或patch权重增强。

Result: 在RoboTwin2.0数据集上，置信度引导选择提升了后训练效率，且基于密集化帧和patch权重的增强进一步提升了预测质量和轨迹一致性，优于奖励、进度或判别器分数等基线方法。

Conclusion: ConfAL-WM方法能够实现对具身世界模型在局部复杂区域的高效后训练优化，为新场景和任务分布下的预测和规划提供了更优的泛化与一致性表现。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models have become an important foundation for embodied prediction, planning, and synthetic data generation, but their errors under new task and scene distributions are often concentrated in localized spatiotemporal regions such as robot arms, manipulated objects, contact areas, and occluded objects. This paper presents ConfAL-WM, a confidence-guided active learning framework for post-training embodied world models. Built upon EVAC, we attach a lightweight confidence probe to UNet decoder features and predict dense confidence maps in the latent space. These maps are aggregated into task-, frame-, and patch-level scores, enabling both efficient data selection and localized training enhancement. Our pipeline first retrains the confidence probe and warms up EVAC with a small subset of target-domain data, then performs task-level prescreening to allocate sampling budgets, and finally applies selected-data retraining with optional frame or patch weighted data enhancement. Experiments on RoboTwin2.0 show that confidence-guided selection improves post-training efficiency, while dense frame and patch weighting further enhances prediction quality and embodied trajectory consistency compared with scalar reward, progress, and judge-based scoring baselines. A quick visual overview of this work is available at https://ConfAL-WM.github.io.

</details>


### [179] [RA-VLA: Retrieval-Augmented VLA for Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25585)
*Sanghwan Jang,Minjin Jeon,Minsoo Kim,Seongjin Choi,Dongha Kim,Hwanjo Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）检索增强框架（RA-VLA），在无需训练的情况下提升机器人任务泛化与适应能力，有效解决现有方法在新任务分布下的适应瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在面对新任务分布时表现脆弱，且主流的无训练方法如In-Context Imitation Learning (ICIL)存在适应瓶颈，难以将专家信息有效转化为执行动作，主要原因是检索机制浅层与模型自身行为惯性。

Method: 提出RA-VLA框架，结合了基于行为对齐的上下文检索机制和扎实的执行流程，通过强化任务相关信息的提取，实现更好的任务适应；并采用可扩展架构保证推理效率。

Result: 在LIBERO基准和实际UR5e机器人环境中，RA-VLA在成功率和计算效率上均优于现有方法，验证了其卓越性能。

Conclusion: RA-VLA为无需训练的机器人任务适应提供了稳健高效的解决方案，解决了现有VLA方法的适应性和效率问题。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models provide a versatile foundation for general robotic manipulation, yet they exhibit significant brittleness when confronted with novel task distributions. While In-Context Imitation Learning (ICIL) offers a training-free alternative, existing frameworks suffer from an adaptation bottleneck that hinders the effective translation of expert context to executable actions. This failure originates from superficial retrieval mechanisms and an inherent behavioral inertia that anchors the policy to its pre-trained priors. To address these limitations, we present RA-VLA, a retrieval-augmented VLA framework that integrates behavior-aligned context retrieval with a grounded execution pipeline. By enforcing faithful adherence to functional cues within a scalable architecture, RA-VLA facilitates seamless task adaptation while preserving inference efficiency. Our empirical evaluations across the LIBERO benchmark and a real-world UR5e environment demonstrate that RA-VLA achieves superior success rates and computational efficiency, establishing a robust framework for training-free robotic adaptation.

</details>


### [180] [Advantage-Driven Explicit Memory for Social Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25610)
*Yeonsoo Park,Mattia Racca,Guillaume Bono,Steeven Janny,Gianluca Monaci,Tomi Silander,Christian Wolf*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种带有非参数化显式记忆的新型导航机器人，通过记忆关键事件，提升了持续学习和泛化能力，特别是在异常罕见但代价高昂事件的应对上具有优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人策略大多仅依赖于网络参数来存储行为，导致表示学习负担过重，且在遇到测试阶段新情况（如人群碰撞）时泛化能力有限，难以持续学习。

Method: 作者提出结合非参数化显式记忆的导航策略，同时集成进入递归PPO架构中，用隐藏状态检索记忆以表达连续时空动态。关键事件通过RL的优势信号由显式记忆记录。训练结合高逼真模拟渲染与群体非视觉模拟，支持收集部署阶段的新数据，不断充实记忆。

Result: 实验表明，加入显式记忆后，代理能更好记住稀疏但代价高昂的失败（如与人碰撞），在处理异常社交行为和应对OOD（分布外）情境时更加稳健，且有助于缓解虚实差距。

Conclusion: 引入非参数化显式记忆的导航策略，有效减轻了对表示学习的依赖，提升了灾难性失败事件的应对能力和泛化性能，为机器人连续学习和真实环境部署提供了有力支持。

Abstract: Robot policies are predominantly learned with classical parametric variants of imitation learning or RL, where training stores the agent's behavior exclusively in the policy's network parameters, putting a heavy burden on the representation learning algorithm. We propose a new navigation agent equipped with non-parametric memory which explicitly indexes prior steps leading to critical events. The advantages are twofold: first, it allows the policy to outsource some of its behavior into an explicit memory; second, it encourages a form of continual learning by allowing an agent to collect data from its testing episodes during deployment and therefore to better generalize to OOD situations. In the context of social navigation, we show that this improves the agent's capability to retain sparse, high-cost failures, such as human collisions. If the policy is trained in simulation, this also naturally addresses the sim-to-real gap, partially, by basing some of the decision making on real data. We integrate the explicit memory into a recurrent PPO architecture and use hidden states for memory retrieval to capture continuous spatiotemporal dynamics. The goal of exploiting rare, high-impact events is achieved by leveraging the RL agent's advantage signals. We train our agent in simulation with a combination of photorealistic rendering and non-visual crowd simulation and show that the agent is robust with respect to OOD social behavior.

</details>


### [181] [Leveraging Inter-object Affordances for Efficient Planning in Contact-rich Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25641)
*Pouya P. Niaz,Justus Piater,Alejandro Agostini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于视觉-语言模型(VLM)的新型任务-运动规划(TAMP)方法，能够利用物体间交互抽象，有效提升机器人处理复杂多物体任务的规划成功率和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统TAMP方法常简化物体模型，忽略了完成接触丰富任务所需的重要物理属性，且在运动规划阶段采用了低效的子符号推理，导致实际应用受限、成功率低、耗时长。

Method: 作者提出了U-TAMP方法，利用VLM生成物体间的物理交互约束（如抓取、支撑），并以此丰富TAMP领域的规划模型，使规划任务能适应多形状、大小、材质物体的复杂物理属性。通过在厨房场景的仿真实验，与原始U-TAMP及现有VLM方法进行对比验证。

Result: 新方法在规划成功率上显著高于对比方法，且在规划耗时上提升1-2个数量级。

Conclusion: 基于VLM生成的物理交互约束极大提升了TAMP在复杂实际场景中的适用性和效率，为面向现实世界的机器人任务规划提供了新思路。

Abstract: Traditional task-and-motion planning (TAMP) approaches primarily focus on defining sequences of actions along with the necessary geometric and kinematic constraints to execute long-horizon tasks. However, their applicability in real-world settings is limited, as they typically assume simplified object models that overlook key physical properties critical for the successful execution of contact-rich tasks. Moreover, they often use sub-symbolic reasoning during motion planning, which drastically increases planning time and decreases overall success rates. We propose a method that leverages a TAMP approach, defining object-centric abstractions of execution constraints, called Unified TAMP (U-TAMP), to execute robotic tasks involving interactions among objects with heterogeneous shapes, sizes, and materials. Using a Vision-Language Model (VLM), we generate abstractions of inter-object affordances for characterizing physical interaction constraints between objects in contact-rich tasks, such as grasp and support constraints. These constraints are used to enrich the U-TAMP planning domain to deal with objects with variable physical properties. We perform experiments in simulated kitchen table organization scenarios and compare our results with those of the original U-TAMP, as well as a state-of-the-art VLM-based planner that leverages common sense knowledge of objects' affordances for plan generation. Our approach achieves significantly higher planning success rates and improves planning times by one to two orders of magnitude compared to other methods.

</details>


### [182] [EgoNav: Bridging Learned Waypoints and Geometry-Aware Local Control for Robust Indoor Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25642)
*Jing Wang,Shiqi Zhao,Hairong Qu,Peng Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoNav是一种结合了语义分割与自适应路径规划的分层图像导航系统，能在室内环境中安全有效地引导机器人移动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于地标图像的导航系统虽然实用，但学习到的航点预测易违背几何约束、脱离主路径，且缺乏适应狭小空间的灵活避障能力。

Method: EgoNav通过语义分割生成可行走区域，结合学习到的航点生成候选目标，并对这些目标按几何安全、方向一致性和与学习先验的吻合度等指标进行评分。然后利用自适应本地路径规划器，动态调整参数来安全执行最终选定的航点。

Result: EgoNav在虚拟仿真（Habitat-sim）与实体机器人测试中，导航成功率和路径效率均优于其他主流方法。

Conclusion: EgoNav成功兼顾了学习型导航预测的直觉与几何及安全性约束，在实际部署中更具实用价值。

Abstract: Image-goal navigation using lightweight topological maps is a practical paradigm for indoor robot deployment: the map requires only geotagged images, and localization relies on visual matching rather than precise pose estimation. However, learned waypoint predictors can produce targets that violate geometric constraints or deviate from the global path. Executing these waypoints safely further requires a local planner capable of collision avoidance, yet existing systems either lack one or rely on fixed parameters that cannot adapt to confined spaces. To address these limitations while retaining the navigational intuition of the learned predictor, we present EgoNav, a hierarchical system that implements this idea by generating candidates from semantically segmented traversable regions and scoring them alongside the learned waypoint for geometric safety, directional coherence, and fidelity to the learned prior. An adaptive local path planner then executes the refined waypoint with parameters modulated based on the refinement outcome. Experiments in Habitat-sim and on a physical humanoid robot show that EgoNav consistently outperforms contemporary baselines in both success rate and path efficiency.

</details>


### [183] [GaussianDream++: Efficient 3D Gaussian World Modeling for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25659)
*Yuqing Jiang,Zijian Zhang,Weitao Zhou,Jiawei Wang,Junjie He,Lei Yang,Haifang Qing,Si Liu,Ding Zhao,Ping Luo,Haibao Yu*
